## Best date plans

### 2026-10-18 — Jad Youssef: Lebnani + Rangoon Brooklyn

**Jad Youssef: Lebnani, then dinner.** Burmese at Rangoon Brooklyn. It is a 1-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 5 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=657+Washington+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&destination=500+Prospect+Pl%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.booksaremagic.net/events) · [Restaurant](https://www.rangoon.nyc/)

### 2026-10-13 — Relief Printmaking + Good Time Country Buffet

**Relief Printmaking, then a meal.** Southern buffet at Good Time Country Buffet. It is 1.8 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 2:30 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Bryant+Park%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10018&destination=121+St+Marks+Pl%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://bryantpark.org/calendar/event/art-cart-workshop-230pm/2026-10-13)

### 2026-10-17 — The Addams Family + Olmsted

**The Addams Family, then a meal.** Seasonal American at Olmsted. It is a 23-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=188+Prospect+Park+W%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=659+Vanderbilt+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/) · [Restaurant](https://www.olmstednyc.com/) · [Reservations](https://www.olmstednyc.com/reservations)

### 2026-10-14 — Long Island Bar + Scientific Controversies: Octopus Umwelt

**Dinner, then Scientific Controversies: Octopus Umwelt.** Cocktail bar and American at Long Island Bar. It is 1.2 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Scientific Controversies: Octopus Umwelt starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.

## Where to eat

Verified the October 13 dining candidates against captured public evidence and limited follow-up checks, keeping useful restaurant-specific cards while removing duplicate or unsupported offers.

Dining research: 2026-10-13T10:35:20+00:00

No retained NYC candidate is described as Michelin-starred. Kirbee's source mentions Michelin recognition for the pitmasters' Texas restaurants/individual award history, not a Michelin star or Michelin Guide status for Kirbee's. Eater heatmaps are editorial selections, not Michelin awards.

### Good Time Country Buffet's all-you-can-eat Southern dinner

More restaurants — Good Time Country Buffet

Eater reported the restaurant opened September 12 from the Kisa and C as in Charlie team, serving fried chicken, collards, mac and cheese, salad bar and rotating buffet dishes; Resy separately confirms dinner-only opening hours and weekly-special ideas.

Price: $26.99 adults; $23.99 age 65+; $13.99 kids ages 4-9; desserts and boozy peach frozen drinks extra  
Schedule: Wednesday-Sunday 5:00-10:00 PM  
Conditions: Dinner prices and hours verified by Eater/Resy: $26.99 adults, $23.99 ages 65+, $13.99 kids ages 4-9; desserts, boozy peach frozen drinks and unlimited sweet tea/soda cost extra. Walk-ins and limited Resy reservations reported. Sources: https://ny.eater.com/news/413774/good-time-country-buffet-east-village-opening-kisa; https://blog.resy.com/2026/09/good-time-country-buffet-nyc/  
Opened: 2026-09-12  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413774/good-time-country-buffet-east-village-opening-kisa)

### Texas pitmaster collaboration lands in Greenpoint

More restaurants — Kirbee's

Eater reported Barbs B Q's Chuck Charnichart and Goldee's Jonny White opened a daytime barbecue spot with two pitmaster perspectives: South Texas/Mexican-influenced plates and more classic brisket, ribs, turkey and sides.

Schedule: First limited service 11:00 AM-2:00 PM; full Labor Day weekend opening reported; current regular hours not verified on official page  
Conditions: Opening timing and first-service hours are from Eater; current recurring hours were not verified because the official domain only returned a cookie/landing shell.
